\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla g^{2}_{R}\left(\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}|\mathbf{V}|^{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\left(g^{2}_{R}|\mathbf{V}|^{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}.

On one hand, we see that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla g^{2}_{R}\left(\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}|\mathbf{V}|^{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}=

\displaystyle-(1+2\beta)\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+\frac{|\mathbf{x}|^{2}}{R^{2}}}\frac{\mathbf{x}\cdot\mathbf{U}}{R^{2}}\mathbf{V}^{2}_{\beta,R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+2\beta}{2R}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}^{2}.

On the other hand, we get by using
\beta\in(0,1)
that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\left(g^{2}_{R}|\mathbf{V}|^{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}=

\displaystyle 2\beta\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{U}\cdot\mathbf{x})\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2(\beta-1)}\left(g^{2}_{R}|\mathbf{V}|^{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta\|\mathbf{U}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thus we have

(2.45)

-\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}\left(g^{2}_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\mathbf{V}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq\frac{C}{R}\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, one has

(2.46)

\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\mathbf{F}_{1R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

While

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|g_{R}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|g_{R}\right\|_{W^{\alpha,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

and this estimate implies

\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Recall that

(2.47)

-\Delta P=-\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{F}_{1}-\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)=:-\Delta P_{1}-\Delta P_{2}-\Delta P_{3}.

Since
\beta\in(0,1)
, the weight
|\mathbf{x}|^{2\beta}
belongs to the
A_{2}
-class, and then we obtain

(2.48)

\begin{split}-\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}g_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\nabla P_{2}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\nabla P_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

We have by the integration by parts that

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}g_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\nabla P_{1}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\left(\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla g^{2}_{R}\right)\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}P_{1}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}+\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\left(\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\right)g^{2}_{R}P_{1}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}.

By the Hölder inequality and the fact the weight
|\mathbf{x}|^{2\beta}
belongs to
A_{2}
-class, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\left(\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla g^{2}_{R}\right)\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}P_{1}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}=

\displaystyle-(1+2\beta)\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+\frac{|\mathbf{x}|^{2}}{R^{2}}}\frac{\mathbf{x}\cdot\mathbf{V}}{R^{2}}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}P_{1}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R^{1-\beta}}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|P_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R^{1-\beta}}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\left(\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}\right)g^{2}_{R}P_{1}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}=

\displaystyle 2\beta\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{V}\cdot\mathbf{x})\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2(\beta-1)}\left(g^{2}_{R}P_{1}\right)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|P_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Therefore

(2.49)

-\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}g_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\nabla P_{1}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq C\|\mathbf{U}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

In the same way as in the proof of (
2.49
), the term involving
P_{3}
can be bounded as follows:

(2.50)

\begin{split}-\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}g_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\nabla P_{3}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\leq&C\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|P_{3}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Inserting estimates (
2.43
)-(
2.50
) into the equality (
2.42
), we readily get that for large
R
and
\forall\,\varepsilon>0,

(2.51)

\begin{split}&\left\|\Lambda^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\frac{e\beta}{2\alpha}\|\mathbf{V}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left(\|\mathbf{V}\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|^{2}_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)\\
&+\varepsilon\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|^{2}_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\frac{C}{R}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|^{2}_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Since
\beta\in(0,1)
, we know that the coefficient
\frac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}>0
and
g_{R}\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}
meets the requirement of test function (
2.42
). Moreover, (
2.51
) becomes

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}_{\beta,R}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{R}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\mathbf{V}\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|^{2}_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

According to this estimate and the following estimate from Theorem
2.13

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\big(\left\|\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big),

we can show the desired estimate (
2.40
) by performing the above process again and sending
R\to\infty.

∎

Theorem 2.15
.

Let
s>0
,
\alpha\in(0,1]
,
\mathbf{F}_{1}\in H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\mathbf{F}_{2}\in H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\mathbf{U}\equiv 0
.
Assume that
(\mathbf{V},P)\in H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\times H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
is a weak solution to the system (
2.26
).

(i)

When
\alpha\in(0,1)
, if

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)<\infty,\,\,\,\forall\beta\in(0,\alpha),

we have

(2.52)

\begin{split}\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\langle\cdot\rangle^{2\beta}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

(ii)

When
\alpha=1
, if

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)<\infty,

we have

(2.53)

\begin{split}\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\langle\cdot\rangle^{2}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Proof.

First of all, we consider the following approximate system in the whole space

(2.54)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}_{N}+\nabla P_{N}=S_{N}\mathbf{F},\\
&\textnormal{div}\,\mathbf{V}_{N}=0.\end{aligned}\ \right.

Since
\mathbf{F}\in H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
, we know that
S_{N}\mathbf{F}\in H^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3}):=\cap_{s\geq 0}H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
belongs to the Schwarz class. Moreover, following the proof of Theorem
2.13
, we can get
\mathbf{V}_{N}\in H^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
is a smooth solution to the system (
2.54
). Performing
differential operator
D^{\gamma}
with
|\gamma|=[s]
to the approximate system yields

(2.55)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}+\nabla D^{\gamma}P_{N}\\
=&D^{\gamma}S_{N}\mathbf{F}+\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=[s]-1}C_{\gamma}^{\gamma_{1}}(D\mathbf{x})\cdot\nabla\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\mathbf{V}_{N},\\
&\operatorname{div}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

Denoting
\{s\}=s-[s]
, one easily verifies that

(2.56)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\\
=&-\nabla\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}P_{N}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}S_{N}\mathbf{F}+\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=[s]-1}C_{\gamma}^{\gamma_{1}}\Lambda^{\{s\}}\big((D\mathbf{x})\cdot\nabla\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\mathbf{V}_{N}\big),\\
&\operatorname{div}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

In terms of Fourier transform, we easily find that

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}\left(\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right)=

\displaystyle|\xi|^{\{s\}}\operatorname{div}_{\xi}\left(\xi\mathscr{F}(D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N})(\xi)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}_{\xi}\left(\xi|\xi|^{\{s\}}\mathscr{F}(D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N})(\xi)\right)-\{s\}|\xi|^{\{s\}}\mathscr{F}(D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N})(\xi).

Plugging this equality into (
2.56
) leads to

(2.57)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}+\nabla\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}P_{N}\\
=&\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}S_{N}\mathbf{F}+\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=[s]-1}C_{\gamma}^{\gamma_{1}}\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left((D\mathbf{x})\cdot\nabla\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)+\frac{\{s\}}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N},\\
&\operatorname{div}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

In particular, when
s\in(0,1),
problem (
2.57
) can be reduced to

(2.58)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}+\nabla\Lambda^{s}P_{N}\\
=&\Lambda^{s}S_{N}\mathbf{F}+\frac{s}{2\alpha}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N},\\
&\operatorname{div}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

Furthermore, we apply (
2.40
) to
\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}
in (
2.58
) to get

(2.59)

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\left[\Lambda^{s},\mathbf{U}\cdot\nabla\right]\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since

\displaystyle\big||\mathbf{x}|^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\big|=

\displaystyle\left||\mathbf{x}|^{\beta}\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}{h}_{N}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})\left(\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right)(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}|^{\beta}\left|{h}_{N}\right|(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})\left|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right|(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}\left|h_{N}\right|(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})|\mathbf{y}|^{\beta}\left|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right|(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y},

we have

(2.60)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C2^{-\beta N}\left\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\||\cdot|^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since

S_{N}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}=\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}+\sum_{k\geq N}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2},

we have by the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sum_{k\geq N}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Furthermore, we calculate

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq N}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq N}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{k}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sum_{k\geq N}\left\|[\dot{\Delta}_{k},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{k\geq N}\left\|[\dot{\Delta}_{k},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{k\geq N}2^{-k}\left\|\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thus, it follows that

(2.61)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting (
2.60
) and (
2.61
) into (
2.59
) yields

(2.62)

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\mathbf{F}_{1}\in H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\mathbf{F}_{2}\in H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
, we get from Theorem
2.13
and Theorem
2.14
that

(2.63)

\left\|\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{s+2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\nabla P_{N}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

and

(2.64)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left(\|\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).\end{split}

For each
s\in(0,1),
we have by Lemma
2.11
that

(2.65)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\Lambda^{s}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting (
2.63
), (
2.64
) and (
2.65
) into (
2.62
), we obtain that for each
s\in[0,\alpha],

(2.66)

\begin{split}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

For each
s\in(\alpha,\min\{1,2\alpha\}),
we have by Lemma
2.11
again that

(2.67)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\Lambda^{s-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging (
2.63
), (
2.66
) and (
2.67
) into (
2.62
) leads to that
for each
s\in(\alpha,\min\{1,2\alpha\}),

\begin{split}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Proceeding step by step, we can show that for each
s\in(0,1),

(2.68)

\begin{split}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{s})\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Next, we consider the case where
s\geq 1
. Suppose there exists an integer
k\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
such that for
0\leq s<k,

(2.69)

\begin{split}\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\Lambda^{s}\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Employing (
2.40
) in the system (
2.55
), we readily have

(2.70)

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

From (
2.60
), it is easy to infer that

(2.71)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C2^{-\beta N}\left\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\||\cdot|^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

The same way as deriving (
2.61
) enables us to conclude that

(2.72)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
k\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, we have by the Leibniz rule that for each
M\in\mathbb{N}^{+},

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq M}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{k}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|S_{M}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-M\alpha}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|S_{M}D\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|S_{M}\left(D\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

which implies

(2.73)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C2^{-M\alpha}\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Inserting (
2.71
)-(
2.73
) into (
2.70
) and then taking the suitable
M
, we get

(2.74)

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

With estimates (
2.63
) and (
2.73
) in hand, the estimate (
2.74
) becomes

(2.75)

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Employing (
2.40
) in the system (
2.57
), we readily have that for each
s\in(k,k+1),

(2.76)

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By mimicking (
2.60
), we can show

(2.77)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C2^{-\beta N}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

From (
2.61
), we get

(2.78)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}S_{N}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

In the similar method as in proof of (
2.73
), we infer that for each
k\in\mathbb{N}^{+},

(2.79)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C2^{-M\alpha}\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Inserting (
2.77
)-(
2.79
) into (
2.70
) and then taking the suitable integer
M
, we immediately get that for each
s\in(k,k+1),

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging (
2.63
) and (
2.73
) into the above inequality leads to that for each
s\in(k,k+1),

(2.80)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k-1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Using the estimate (
2.75
), we get from (
2.80
) that for each
s\in[k,k+1),

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=k}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By induction, we eventually get that for each
s\geq 0,

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}_{N}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Taking
N\to\infty
implies the desired estimate (
2.52
).

In the same way as in the proof of (
2.52
), we can show the desired estimate (
2.53
) of Theorem
2.15
.
∎

3.
High Regularity for weak solutions to Leray problem

In this section, we are going to show the high regularity for weak solutions to Leray problem (
1.1
) and the perturbed Leray problem (
1.6
). For simplicity, we just need to consider the following hybrid problem in the whole space

(3.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{V}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}+\nabla P=-\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}+L_{\mathbf{U}_{0}}(\mathbf{V})+\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F},\\
&\textnormal{div}\,\mathbf{V}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

where

L_{\mathbf{U}_{0}}\mathbf{V}=-\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}.

Let
\mathbf{F}_{1}=-\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}+\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}.
Then the system (
3.1
) can be rewritten as

(3.2)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\mathbf{V}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{V}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}+(\mathbf{U}_{0}+\mathbf{V})\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}+\nabla P=\mathbf{F}_{1},\\
&\textnormal{div}\,\mathbf{V}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

3.1.
High regularity for weak solutions to the perturbed Leray problem

This subsection is devoted to proving
L^{p}
-type elliptic regularity for the weak solutions to the problem (
3.1
).

Theorem 3.1
.

Assume
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
,
p\in[2,+\infty)
,
\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
. Let
(\mathbf{V},P)\in H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\times L^{\frac{3}{3-2\alpha}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
be a weak solution to the system (
3.1
). Then there exists a constant
C=C_{p,\sigma}>0
such that

(3.3)

\left\|\mathbf{U}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{\frac{5}{3}}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p,\sigma}\left(1+\left\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

Proof.

Let
\mathbf{V}_{k}=J_{k}\mathbf{V}
be a sequence of smooth functions approximating
\mathbf{V}
, and
\mathbf{W}_{k}\in H^{\alpha}_{\sigma}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
be a weak solution to the corresponding linearized equations in the whole space

(3.4)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{k}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{k}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{W}_{k}+J_{k}\big((\mathbf{U}_{0}+\mathbf{V}_{k})\cdot\nabla J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\big)+\nabla P_{k}=\mathbf{F}_{1},\\
&\textnormal{div}\,\mathbf{W}_{k}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

Since
\mathbf{U}_{0}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3}),\,\mathbf{V}\in H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
, we readily have that
\mathbf{F}_{1}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3}),
moreover, we get the following estimate by the standard
L^{2}
-estimate

(3.5)

\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{\alpha}\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Taking
s=0
in Theorem
2.13
, we find that weak solution
(\mathbf{W}_{k},P_{k})
fulfils

(3.6)

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C_{p}\left(\left\|\operatorname{div}\left((\mathbf{U}_{0}+\mathbf{V}_{k})\otimes J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\right)\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).\end{split}

Let us first consider the case where
p\in[2,{9}/{2})
. Since
\mathbf{V}\in H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
with
\alpha\in[5/6,1],
we obtain by Lemma
2.7
that for each
p\in[2,{9}/{2})
,

(3.7)

\|\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{V}_{k}\otimes J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\right)\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\varepsilon\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{\frac{5}{3}}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\alpha\geq\frac{5}{6}
, one has by using the interpolation inequality and the Young inequality that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{\frac{5}{3}}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|^{\frac{6\alpha-5}{6\alpha}}_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|^{\frac{5}{6\alpha}}_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\alpha}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

This estimate together with (
3.7
) leads to

(3.8)

\|\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{V}_{k}\otimes J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\right)\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\varepsilon\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Using the Leibniz estimate, we see that

(3.9)

\begin{split}\|\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\right)\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Since
\alpha\in[5/6,1],
we get by the interpolation inequality and the Young inequality that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|^{\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}}_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|^{\frac{1}{2\alpha}}_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\varepsilon\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging this estimate into (
3.9
) leads to

(3.10)

\|\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes J_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\right)\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\varepsilon\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting estimates (
3.8
) and (
3.10
) into (
3.6
), we have

(3.11)

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C_{p}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)+\varepsilon C_{p,\mathbf{U}_{0}}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Since
p\geq 2,
we obtain the following sharp inequality

(3.12)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{{B}^{0}_{p,1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&\sum_{q\leq N}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{W}_{k}\big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sum_{q>N}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{W}_{k}\big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C2^{\frac{p-2}{2p}N}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+2^{-2\alpha N}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},\end{split}

where
N
is an integer to be chosen later.

Plugging this sharp inequality into estimate (
3.11
) allows us to conclude

(3.13)

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C_{p}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+2^{\frac{p-2}{2p}N}\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)+C_{p}\left(\varepsilon+C_{\varepsilon}2^{-\frac{5}{3}N}\right)\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{\dot{B}^{\frac{5}{3}}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Choosing a suitable number
\varepsilon
and a sufficiently large integer
N
in (
3.13
), one has

(3.14)

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

Plugging (
3.5
) into (
3.14
), we immediately obtain

(3.15)

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

According to
\mathbf{F}_{1}=-\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}+\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F},
we have by the Hölder inequality that

(3.16)

\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

and by the Leibniz estimate that for each
p\in[2,9/2),

(3.17)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\|_{\dot{F}^{0}_{p,2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Plugging (
3.16
) and (
3.17
) into (
3.15
), we immediately have that for each
p\in[2,9/2),

(3.18)

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p,\sigma}\left(1+\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

The above uniform estimate (
3.18
) uniformly in
k
enables us to infer that subsequences of
\mathbf{W}_{k}
and
P_{k}

converge locally weakly in
H^{2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and in
H^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
to a solution
(\mathbf{W},P)
of Cauchy problem

(3.19)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &(-\Delta)^{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{2\alpha-1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathbf{x}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{W}+\big(\mathbf{U}_{0}+\mathbf{V}\big)\cdot\nabla\mathbf{W}+\nabla P=\mathbf{F}_{1}\\
&\textnormal{div}\,\mathbf{W}=0.\end{aligned}\ \right.

Since
\mathbf{V}
and
\mathbf{W}
are both weak solutions to the equations (
3.19
), we get by uniqueness that
\mathbf{V}=\mathbf{W},
and we have

(3.20)

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{\frac{5}{3}}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p,\sigma}\left(1+\left\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

Next we consider the case where
\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
with
\frac{9}{2}\leq p<\infty
. By the interpolation theorem, we have that for each
r\in(2,p),

\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{r,r}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|^{\frac{2(p-r)}{r(p-2)}}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|^{\frac{(r-2)p}{r(p-2)}}_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

which implies that

\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
for each
p\in[2,9/2)
. We know from (
3.20
) that for each
p\in[2,9/2)
,

\mathbf{V}\in H^{2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\quad\text{ with }\alpha\in[5/6,1].

Hence, by using the Sobolev embedding theorem, we have
\mathbf{V}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and

\nabla\mathbf{V}\in L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\quad\text{ for each }p\in[2,\infty).

These facts imply the Leibniz estimate

(3.21)

\begin{split}&\left\|(\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\cdot\nabla\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left(\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)\left\|\nabla\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+\left\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}<\infty.\end{split}

Since
\mathbf{V}
is weak solution to the system (
3.1
) with
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
, we have by Theorem
2.13
that

(3.22)

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C_{p}\left(\left\|\operatorname{div}\left((\mathbf{U}_{0}+\mathbf{V})\otimes\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{W}_{k}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).\end{split}

Inserting (
3.12
) and (
3.21
) into (
3.22
) and then choosing the suitable
N
, we finally obtain
Theorem
3.1
for the case where
p>\frac{9}{2}
.
∎

Theorem 3.2
.

Assume
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
,
s\geq 0
,
p\in[2,+\infty)
,
\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
and
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
.
Let
\mathbf{V}
be a weak solution to the system (
3.1
). Then there exists a constant
C
such that

(3.23)

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left(p,\sigma,\|\operatorname{div}\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

Proof.

Applying Theorem
2.13
to the equations (
3.1
), we get that for all
s>0,

(3.24)

\begin{split}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{p}\left(\left\|\overline{\mathbf{F}}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right),\end{split}

where

\overline{\mathbf{F}}=-\operatorname{div}\left((\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\otimes\mathbf{V}\right)-\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{V}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0}\right)+\Div\mathbf{F}.

Since
\Div\mathbf{F}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
with
s\geq 0
, we have by the interpolation theorem that there exists
\theta=\frac{2ps}{2ps+3(p-2)}
such that

\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|^{\theta}_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|^{1-\theta}_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}<\infty.

Moreover, it follows from Theorem
3.1
that

(3.25)

\mathbf{V}\in H^{2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3}).

Thanks to the Bony paraproduct decomposition, we have by the Leibniz estimates that

\displaystyle\left\|\operatorname{div}\left((\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\otimes\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{\dot{W}^{s+1,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{V}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0}\right)\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Both estimates above imply

\displaystyle\|\overline{\mathbf{F}}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}}\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{\dot{W}^{s+1,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting this estimate into (
3.24
) yields

(3.26)

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C_{p}\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{\dot{W}^{s+1,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{p}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Since
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2}),
we have by Lemma
2.8
that for each
s\geq 0
,

(3.27)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\|\Delta_{-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sum_{q\geq 0}e^{-2^{qs}}\|\Delta_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\left\|1/|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\end{split}

for each
p>\frac{3}{2\alpha-1}
and

(3.28)

\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{q,1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|_{W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\left\|1/|\cdot|^{2\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\quad\text{for each}\,\,\,q>\frac{3}{2\alpha}.

Thanks to (
3.25
), (
3.27
) and (
3.28
), we easily find by condition
\overline{\mathbf{F}}\in L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
that all terms in the right side of (
3.26
) are bound as long as
s\leq 1-\alpha
, that is, for each
0\leq s\leq 1-\alpha,

(3.29)

\left\|\mathbf{U}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq C\left(p,\sigma,\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

With estimate (
3.29
) in hand, we can show by repeating the above step that
for each
0\leq s\leq 2(1-\alpha),

\left\|\mathbf{U}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+2\alpha}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq C\left(p,\sigma,\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

Repeat the above process until the termination condition of Theorem
3.2
is reached.
∎

Taking
\sigma=0
in Theorem
3.1
and Theorem
3.2
, they become Theorem
1.4
, (ii)-(1). Now we come back to show the high regularity of weak solutions in Theorem
1.8
. Since
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2}),

we have by the Leibniz estimate, (
3.27
) and (
3.28
) that for each
s\geq 0
and
p\geq 2,

\displaystyle\left\|\Div(\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0})\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{p,p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{3p}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{3p/2,1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{L^{3p/2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{s}_{3p,1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}<\infty.

Taking
\Div\mathbf{F}=\Div(\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0})
in Theorem
3.1
and Theorem
3.2
, we immediately obtain the high regularity of weak solutions in Theorem
1.8
.

3.2.
Regularity in the framework of the weighted Hilbert space

In this subsection, we will study the regularity in the framework of the weighted Hilbert space, which helps us to analysis the behaviour of weak solution for large
|\mathbf{x}|.

Theorem 3.3
.

Let
s\geq 0,

\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
and
\mathbf{F}\in H^{s+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
. Assume that
\mathbf{V}
is a weak solution to the system (
3.1
). Then there exists a constant
C
such that

(i)

if
\alpha\in[\frac{5}{6},1],

\Div\mathbf{F}\in H^{s}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}}
, we have that for each
\beta\in(0,\alpha),

(3.30)

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+\alpha}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{H^{s+\alpha}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\Big(\sigma,\left\|\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{H^{s}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2\beta}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\Big);

(ii)

if
\alpha=1,

\Div\mathbf{F}\in H^{s}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2}}
, we have

(3.31)

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|P\right\|_{H^{s+1}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\Big(\sigma,\left\|\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{H^{s}_{\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{2}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\Big).

Proof.

First of all, we have from Theorem
3.1
and Theorem
3.2
that for each
s\geq 0,

(3.32)

\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s+2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\nabla P\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
, we have that

\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})\cap\dot{W}^{1,\frac{3}{\alpha}}(\mathbf{R}^{3}).

By Theorem
2.14
, we readily have

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right).

This estimate together with the fact
\mathbf{U}_{0}=\frac{\sigma}{|\mathbf{x}|^{2\alpha-1}}
and
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
implies

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C,

which together with (
3.32
) gives

(3.33)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{\sigma}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thanks to Lemma
2.11
and (
3.32
), it follows from (
3.33
) that

(3.34)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\alpha})\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C_{\sigma}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Next, we consider the case where
s>0.
By Theorem
2.15
, we get for each
s>0,

(3.35)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left(\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)\\
&+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

where

\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}=-\operatorname{div}\left(\mathbf{V}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbf{F}_{2}=-(\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\otimes\mathbf{V}.

Since
\alpha\in[5/6,1],
we have by the Leibniz estimate and (
3.32
) that

(3.36)

\begin{split}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&C\left\|(\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\otimes\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left(\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{W^{1+s,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\right)\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{1+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C_{\sigma}\|\Div\mathbf{F}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Now we deal with the term involving
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}
. For the case
\{s\}=0
, it is obvious that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}=

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

The Leibniz formula enables us to conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{s}\sup_{{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=s}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\|_{H^{s-1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

As for the case
\{s\}\in(0,1)
, we know by the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By Lemma
2.11
, we get

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|[\Lambda^{\{s\}},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

According to the Leibniz formula, we get by Leibniz estimates that

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{[s]}\sup_{{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=[s]}}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

and

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Hence, we have that for each
s>0
,

(3.37)

\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting (
3.36
) and (
3.37
) into (
3.35
) sand using
\Div\mathbf{F}\in H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
, we get

(3.38)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\\
&+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Since

\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}=-\langle\mathbf{x}\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0},

we get by the Leibniz estimate that for each
s\geq 0,

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{W^{s,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma\|_{W^{s+1,\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Hence, we have

(3.39)

\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

We turn to deal with the term involving
\mathbf{F}_{2}.
As long as
\{s\}=0
, we see that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=s}\left\|D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{s}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=s}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By the Hölder inequality, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{s}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=s}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{s}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=s}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Therefore, we have that for the case
\{s\}=0
,

(3.40)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

For the case
\{s\}\in(0,1),
we observe that

(3.41)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Div\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2})\right)\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\nabla\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\cdot\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\nabla\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\cdot\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

By the Hölder inequality, we get

(3.42)

\left\|\nabla\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\cdot\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\left\|\nabla\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By Lemma
2.11
, we obtain

(3.43)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta},\Lambda^{\{s\}}]D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\Lambda^{1-\alpha},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\left(\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
&+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\left(\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Our task is now to bound the first term in the last line of (
3.43
). By Lemma
2.11
again, we have that for each
\{s\}\in(0,\alpha),

(3.44)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta},\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}]D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Similarly, we can show that

(3.45)

\begin{split}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\left(\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta},\Lambda^{\{s\}}]D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Inserting (
3.44
) and (
3.45
) into (
3.43
) leads to that for each
\{s\}\in(0,\alpha),

(3.46)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

In terms of the Leibniz rule, one has

(3.47)

\begin{split}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq&\left\|D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{[s]}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=[s]}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Also, we have

(3.48)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{[s]}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=[s]}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Inserting (
3.47
) and (
3.48
) into (
3.46
) yields that for each
\{s\}\in(0,\alpha),

(3.49)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Now we consider the case where
\{s\}\in[\alpha,1),
which means
1+\{s\}-\alpha\geq 1
. In terms of Lemma
2.12
, we immediately have

(3.50)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\ \leq C\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Moreover, we have by Lemma
2.11
that

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By the Leibniz rule, one has

(3.51)

\begin{split}&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{[s]+1}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}-\alpha}\left(\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},\end{split}

and

(3.52)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{[s]+1}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting (
3.51
) and (
3.52
) into (
3.50
), we have that for each
\{s\}\in[\alpha,1),

(3.53)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{1+\{s\}-\alpha}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging (
3.42
), (
3.49
) and (
3.53
) into (
3.41
) yields that for each
\{s\}\in(0,1),

(3.54)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1+s-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Collecting both estimates (
3.40
) and (
3.54
), we obtain that for each
s>0,

(3.55)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\mathbf{F}_{2}=-(\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{U}_{0})\otimes\mathbf{V},
we have

(3.56)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\otimes\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

and

(3.57)

\left\|\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{W^{s,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thanks to the Leibniz estimate, we get by using the fact
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
that

(3.58)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\otimes\mathbf{V}\right\|_{{H}^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s+2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

Also, we have

(3.59)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{V}\right\|_{{H}^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\|_{H^{s+2\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging (
3.58
) and (
3.59
) into (
3.56
) leads to

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Inserting this estimate and (
3.57
) into (
3.55
) and then using (
3.32
), we get

(3.60)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\Div\mathbf{F}_{2}\right\|_{H^{-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C.

Then we put (
3.39
) and (
3.60
) into (
3.38
) to yield

(3.61)

\begin{split}&\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+1-\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\Div\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C.\end{split}

By the same argument as used in the proof of (
3.49
), we can show that

(3.62)

\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}(I_{d}+\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma})\mathbf{F}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{F}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\left\|\mathbf{F}\right\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thanks to the definition of the inhomogeneous Hilbert space, one has

(3.63)

\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

In terms of the Leibniz rule, we find that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sum_{|\gamma_{1}|=1}^{[s+\alpha]}\sup_{|\gamma_{1}|+|\gamma_{2}|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\{s+\alpha\}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s+\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

For
\{s\}\in[0,1-\alpha),
we have
[s+\alpha]=[s]
and

\alpha\leq\{\alpha+s\}=\alpha+\{s\}<1.

By Lemma
2.11
, we further get

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\Lambda^{\alpha+\{s\}},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\left\|[\Lambda^{\alpha},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}D^{\gamma}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s+\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Plugging this estimate into (
3.63
) yields that for each
\{s\}\in[0,1-\alpha),

(3.64)

\begin{split}&\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{s+\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\\
\leq&C\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C\|\mathbf{V}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

For the case where
\{s\}\in[\alpha,1),
we have

[s+\alpha]=[s]+1\quad\text{and}\quad\{s+\alpha\}=\{s\}+\alpha-1.

In view of Lemma
2.11
and Lemma
2.12
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s+\alpha]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s+\alpha\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]+1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\alpha+\{s\}-1}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]+1}\left\|[\Lambda^{\alpha+\{s\}-1},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]+1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By Lemma
2.11
again, one has

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{\alpha}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|[\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta},\Lambda^{\alpha}]\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

By Lemma
2.11
and Lemma
2.12
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]+1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\{s\}}\left(\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|[\Lambda^{1-\{s\}},\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}]\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

From this estimate, it follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]+1}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{|\gamma|=[s]}\left\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\beta}\Lambda^{\{s\}}D^{\gamma}\mathbf{V}\right\|_{H^{1-\{s\}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Furthermore, we get by the fact
\alpha\in[5/6,1]
that for each
\{s\}\in[\alpha,1),
