\displaystyle\left\|\dot{T}_{|\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}}|\cdot|^{2}\Div\mathbf{f}_{2}\right\|_{\dot{B}^{\gamma}_{\infty,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q\gamma}\left\|\dot{S}_{q-1}\big(|\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}\big)\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\Div\mathbf{f}_{2}\big)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\Div\mathbf{f}_{2}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\Div\mathbf{f}_{2}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}=:{\rm A}_{1}+{\rm A}_{2}.

Since
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
and
\mathbf{f}_{2}=\Div(\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0})
, we have by Proposition 4.1 established in
[
11
]
that

(4.64)

{\rm A}_{2}\leq C\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbf{R}^{3}}|\mathbf{x}|^{2\alpha-1}|\mathbf{U}_{0}|(\mathbf{x})\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbf{R}^{3}}|\mathbf{x}|^{2\alpha}|\Div\mathbf{U}_{0}|(\mathbf{x})\left\||\cdot|^{3-4\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}<\infty.

It remains for us to bound
{\rm A}_{2}
. Since
\mathbf{f}_{2}=-\mathbf{U}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{0},
we rewrite it in terms of the Bony paraproduct that

\mathbf{f}_{2}=-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\left(\dot{T}_{\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i}}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}+\dot{T}_{\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i}+\dot{R}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}\right)\right).

Moreover, taking Fourier transform and property of support, we observe that

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\Div\mathbf{f}_{2}\big)\right)(\xi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varphi\left({\xi}/{2^{q}}\right)\Delta_{\xi}\left(i\xi\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{2}(\xi)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\sum_{|k-q|\leq 5}\varphi\left({\xi}/{2^{q}}\right)\Delta_{\xi}\Big(i\xi_{i}\widehat{\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i}}(\xi)\widehat{\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}}(\xi)+i\xi_{i}\widehat{\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}}(\xi)\widehat{\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i}}(\xi)\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\sum_{k\geq q-5}\varphi\left({\xi}/{2^{q}}\right)\Delta_{\xi}\Big(i\xi_{i}\widehat{\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{i}}(\xi)\widehat{\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{j}}(\xi)\Big).

Furthermore, we have by the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{|k-q|\leq 5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{|k-q|\leq 5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-3+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{1}+{\rm A}_{1}^{2}+{\rm A}_{1}^{3}.

To bound these terms, we need the following lemma.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
m\in(0,\infty),

1<r\leq p<\infty
and
f\in L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})
satisfying

\||\cdot|^{m}f\|_{L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}<\infty.

Then there holds that

(4.65)

\big\||\cdot|^{m}\dot{\Delta}_{q}f\big\|_{L^{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{m}f\big)\big\|_{L^{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C2^{-q(m-3(1/r-1/p))}\|f\|_{L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Proof.

First of all, we observe that

|\mathbf{x}|^{m}\dot{\Delta}_{q}f=2^{m}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{m}f\big)+\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}h_{q}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})\left(|\mathbf{x}|^{m}-2^{m}|\mathbf{y}|^{m}\right)f(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}.

It is obvious by using the fact
(a+b)^{p}\leq 2^{p}(a^{p}+b^{p})
with
p\geq 1
that

(4.66)

\begin{split}I:=&\left|\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}h_{q}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})\left(|\mathbf{x}|^{m}-2^{m}|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}|^{m}\right)f(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\right|\\
\leq&2^{m}2^{3q}\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}|^{m}|h|\big(2^{q}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})\big)|f|(\mathbf{y})\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}.\end{split}

Moreover, by the Young inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|I\right\|_{L^{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-q(m-3(1/r-1/p))}\big\||\cdot|^{m}h\big\|_{L^{s}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|f\|_{L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-q(m-3(1/r-1/p))}\|f\|_{L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

where
s
satisfies
1+\frac{1}{p}=\frac{1}{s}+\frac{1}{r}.

Thus, it follows the desired estimate by the triangle inequality that

\big\||\cdot|^{m}\dot{\Delta}_{q}f\big\|_{L^{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{m}f\big)\big\|_{L^{p,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}+C2^{-q(m-3(1/r-1/p))}\|f\|_{L^{r,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

And then we complete the proof of Lemma
4.4
.
∎

By (
4.65
) in Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{|k-q|\leq 5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{|k-q|\leq 5}\left\||\cdot|^{2}\dot{S}_{k-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}=:{\rm A}_{1}^{1,1}+{\rm A}_{1}^{1,2}.

Since

\displaystyle\big\||\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\dot{S}_{q-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{k\leq q-1}\big\||\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{k\leq q-1}2^{-k(2\alpha-1)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{k}\left({\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})},

we have by (
2.2
) and Lemma
2.8
that

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{1,2}\leq

\displaystyle C\big\||\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\dot{S}_{q-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\big\||\cdot|^{3-2\alpha}\nabla\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q2(\alpha-1)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big({\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

By the Hölder inequality, (
2.2
) and Lemma
2.8
, we find that

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{1,1}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\big\||\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\dot{S}_{q-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\||\cdot|^{2\alpha+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\nabla\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\big\||\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}\dot{S}_{q-1}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{2q(\alpha-1)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big({\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3}}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

Collecting estimates for
{\rm A}_{1}^{1,1}
and
{\rm A}_{1}^{1,2}
, we readily have

(4.67)

{\rm A}_{1}^{1}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

Similarly, we can show

(4.68)

{\rm A}_{1}^{2}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

Now we focus on the term
{\rm A}_{1}^{3}
. By (
4.65
) in Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\left(|\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\big(\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q2(1-\alpha)}\left\||\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3,1}+{\rm A}_{1}^{3,2}.

By Lemma
2.8
, we have that for each
\alpha\in[5/6,1),

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3,2}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q2(1-\alpha)}\left\|\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q2(1-\alpha)}\left\||\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q(1-2\alpha)}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

As for
\alpha=1,
we observe by (
4.65
) in Lemma
4.4
that

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3}=

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q(1+\gamma)}\left\||\cdot|^{1+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{3,\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3,1}+\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q}\left\||\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}=:{\rm A}_{1}^{3,1}+{\rm A}_{1}^{3,3}.

By the discrete Young inequality and the Hölder inequality and Lemma
2.8
, we have

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3,3}\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q}\left\|\big(|\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{q}\left\||\cdot|^{2}\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-q}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{q}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Since
\mathbf{u}_{0}(\mathbf{x})=\frac{\sigma}{|\mathbf{x}|}
as
\alpha=1
and
\sigma\in C^{1,0}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
, we have by the Leibniz formula that

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\|1/|\cdot|^{2}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\|\sigma\|_{\dot{W}^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\|1/|\cdot|^{2}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Thus, we have

{\rm A}_{1}^{3,3}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{{W}^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

In view of (
4.65
) in Lemma
4.4
, we see that

\displaystyle{\rm A}_{1}^{3,1}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\geq q-5}2^{q(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{q}\big(|\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\big(\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\big)\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{k(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\big\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\big(\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big)\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{k(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\big\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}.

Moreover, we have by Lemma
2.8
and the Bernstein inequality in Lemma
2.1
that

\displaystyle\big\||\cdot|^{4\alpha-1+\gamma}\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\nabla\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{4\alpha-3},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-k(4\alpha-1+\gamma)}2^{k(3-2\alpha)}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-k(4\alpha-1+\gamma)}2^{k(3-2\alpha)}\big\|\dot{\Delta}_{k}\mathbf{u}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}\big\|\widetilde{\dot{\Delta}}_{k}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-k(4\alpha-3+\gamma)}\left\|{\sigma}/{|\cdot|^{2\alpha-1}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{3}{2\alpha-1},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{3})},

which helps us to infer that

{\rm A}_{1}^{3,1}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

This estimate together with estimates for
{\rm A}_{1}^{3,2}
and
{\rm A}_{1}^{3,3}
leads to

(4.69)

{\rm A}_{1}^{3}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

Collecting estimates (
4.67
), (
4.68
) and (
4.69
), we immediately have

{\rm A}_{1}\leq C\|\sigma\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

This inequality together with (
4.63
) and (
4.64
) yields the claim (
4.61
). So, we complete the proof of Theorem
1.8
.
